E00019        Trader does not exist                                                        a]Trader file has not got 00 set against Vat Reg code. For Pseudo TURNs 
                                                                                        effective date of TURN is before date of entry.

                                                                                        error. Entry must be cancelled in order to use the new Pseudo number 
                                                                                        SAD H – Could be that a turn is being declared in box 2 of an import entry in

                                                                                                    be a turn that will be reconised by CHIEF.

                                                                                                    (CPC) criteria. £600 is the limit

                                                                                                    Code (CPC).

                                                                                                    of the Tariff . 
E00030          CPC's must all be for the same CPC series                                           Only items to the same / similar regimes can be entered together on the 
                                                                                                    same entry. Display the CPC, on the first screen is a CPC series field with a 
                                                                                                    number in it. All items must have the same number on their CPC's to be 
                                                                                                    declared together. 
E00031          CPC & Consignor incompatible                                                        Customs Procedure Code (CPC) restricted to certain importers. TURNs limit

                                                                                                    incorrect contact the Policy Division for the regime, otherwise use the correct 
                                                                                                    TURN or CPC.

E00073         Preference invalid for Agreement Preference                                   Incompatibility between preference code in Box 36 (Preference) and customs 
                                                                                            duty rate in Box 47c (Calculation of Taxes), eg for a Commodity Code with a 
                                                                                            customs duty rate under a preferential agreement in Box 47c (Calculation of 
                                                                                            Taxes) ie 'A' a preference code of 300 would be appropriate. A list of 
                                                                                            commodity preferences can be found in Customs & Excise Tariff, Volume 3, 
                                                                                            Part 3 or on CHIEF using role RLAUDIT, transaction 'LTBE' (List Table) and 
                                                                                            key 'PREF' in table entry field.

E00076          Preference incompatible with Tax Rate                                               Incompatible information recorded in Box 36 (Preference) and Box 47c 
                                                                                                (Calculation of Taxes), ie code 200 in Box 36 (Preferances) Generalised 
                                                                                                System of Preferences (GSP) is not comparible to paying the full rate of 
                                                                                                customs duty in Box 47c (Calculation of Taxes). 
E00077          Preference invalid for Suspension Tax Rate                                          Incompatibility between preference code in Box 36 (Preference) and customs 
                                                                                                duty rate in Box 47c (Calculation of Taxes), eg For a tariff suspension subject 
                                                                                                to a specific End Use, 'F' - 'K' in Box 47c, a preference code of 115 would be 
                                                                                                required in Box 36 (Preferences). Check Tariff, for a list of community 
                                                                                                preference codes (pg 46) or using role RLAUDIT on CHIEF through 
                                                                                                tranasction 'LTBE' (List Table) and key 'PREF' in table entry field. 
E00078          Preference invalid for End Use CPC                                                 Preference code entered in Box 36 (Preference) is not compatible with End 
                                                                                                Use Customs Procedure Code (CPC), eg code 100 (normal tariff 
                                                                                                arrangement - no preference certificate) is not compatible with End Use CPC 
                                                                                                (7 numeric) . Check Tariff, page 46 for list of community Preference codes or 
                                                                                                using role RLAUDIT on CHIEF, transaction 'LTBE' (List Table) and key PREF 
                                                                                                in table type field. 
E00079          Preference incompatible with Quota Declaration                                     Generated on multi item entry when quota items are recorded prior to non 
                                                                                                quota items. As a interim workround, reversal of sequence ie non quota 
                                                                                                items prior to quota items, or insertion of a seperate entry for quota/non 
                                                                                                quota items will allow validation. 
E00081          Preference incompatible with Quota and End Use CPC                                  Incompatibility between preference code entered in Box 36 (Preference) and 
                                                                                                claim to Box 39 (Quota) using an End Use Customs Procedure Code (CPC) 
                                                                                                in Box 37 (7 numeric (CPC). If claim to quota using an End Use CPC is 
                                                                                                correct, check list of Preference codes in Tariff, pg 46. A preferential 
                                                                                                agreement code will commence with a number 3. 
E00083          Preference incompatible with Quota and OPR CPC                                     Check entry in Box 36 (Preference) is compatible with Customs Procedure 
                                                                                                Code (CPC) and quota.

                                                                                                Box 36 (Preference) must be completed with any claim to preference details 
                                                                                                even if you are not claiming. Guidance can be found in the Tariff pg 46.

                                                                                                authorised to use the Consignee's guarantee account.  From the entry details 
                                                                                                write down the Consignee's security lodged reference number.  Do the 
                                                                                                transaction 'DSEC' (Display Security Account Details) type the Security 
                                                                                                Number exactly as it appears on the entry.  Now do transaction 'DSSE' 
                                                                                    (Display Standing Data Security Account) type in the security lodged 
                                                                                    reference number again you will then see all the Declarant/Agent TURNs that 
                                                                                    are authorised to use the Consignee's guarantee account.

E01383          Customs Duty declaration incorrect                                              Check measure on Commodity Code setting through transaction 'DCOM' 
                                                                                            (Display Commodity). Preference rate may be deleted or absent etc. Duty type 
                                                                                            may not apply, it may be steel duty '112' or customs duty type 'A20' for 
                                                                                            agricultural goods. Check also Anti-Dumping Duty. If agent/ importer is using a 
                                                                                            quota or claiming  a preference, check Box 47c (Calculation of Taxes) tax rate 
                                                                                            is either 'A' or 'G' for Preference, or 'F' for a claim to a Quota. 
                                                                                            Try code A20 for  for DTY override 
                                                                                            See SAD H Tariff appendix C7A        for new Tax Type codes . Also check that 
                                                                                            the 4 digit code i.e A999 is being declared with the correct Country Code.

                                                                                            For CAP tax types nul rate tax identifier on measure should be 'Y' not 'N' as 
                                                                                            tax rate should not be declared for CAP measures and for Excise measures if 
                                                                                            EC SUPP then also required on Tariff Measure. Also Check whether Country 
                                                                                            Of Origin (COO) of entry is part of a group for which CAP charges apply.

                                                                                            line as it is below the MIP band therefore A20 no MOP is required.

E02776        CPC and End Use Commodity incompatible                                      Only an End Use commodity code can be used with an End Use CPC and 
                                                                                        conversely a non End Use commodity code must be used with a non End 
                                                                                        Use CPC. End Use and non End Use on CPC and commodity code are 
                                                                                        prohibited combinations. Where a warehouse regime is involved if the 
                                                                                        product is being warehoused for excise duty purposes only unlisted CPC 
                                                                                        0740000 can be used. If the product is going into a customs warehouse, 
                                                                                        another similar products commodity code should be declared on the entry 
                                                                                        and the End Use relief claimed on removal from warehouse.

E10340        Data element error                                                     a] Generated on a UKCTRL message as a result of a EDI CUSDEC 
                                                                                    message received by CHIEF that has failed validation.  The UCR/UCD 
                                                                                    segments on the UKCTRL message identify which segment and element on 
                                                                                    the CUSDEC is causing the error.  The "UCR" number identifies the line* of 
                                                                                    the DATA ELEMENT that has the error and can be located by counting 
                                                                                        down from the segment name "UNH" within the MESSAGE HEADER. * The 
                                                                                        line count can be misleading if e.g. an' has been used in the misc. text field 
                                                                                        as this character is a recognised segment terminator for each line.  The 
                                                                                        "UCD" numbers identify the components of a particular Data element that 
                                                                                        contains the error i.e. UCD+ 1+10 means a count along to the 10th Data 
                                                                                        element component, excluding CHIEF data, the error should be within the 
                                                                                        next element. 
                                                                                        b] Ensure no edifact lines end with a colon.

E10419        Data validation primary error                                            a] This has almost always been associated with a software error in the 
                                                                                    traders EDI system, following the omission of a data segment. Ask trader to 
                                                                                            input an HCI entry at least to VALI then copy details to EDI. If error then 
                                                                                            persists on EDI they should bring to the attention of their software house. 
                                                                                            b] Alternatively the error could be caused by an alpha character in a numeric 
                                                                                            field or vice versa.

                                                                                            particular segment eg Licence Reference is limited to 30. 
                                                                                            d] If it is referring to the AI statement then ensure the types are around the 
                                                                                            right way, ie 01 ROY is correct.
